- **coalface**: Runs a decomposable task's fan-out as a disciplined factory: mandatory scout, deterministic partition, anchor-edit orders as text, QC at collection, single-writer apply behind a snapshot, post-run receipt. Wallet invariant: the swarm's DOLLAR cost stays ~solo via cheap worker tiers (raw tokens run HIGHER — fan-out ×N the per-sub baseline; ~5.3× on a small benchmark), never fewer tokens. Honest frame: it disciplines fan-outs that would happen anyway — it does not make models smarter or guarantee correctness.
